SCOPE 53.3→53.5)\n**Envelope**: ~8K main inline (LIGHT-MEDIUM; investigation + fix + smoke + close)\n\n---\n\n## Pre-flight\n\n- Same-conversation continuation post-S110 (user \"continue\" keep-alive).\n- Sync-grilling NOT due (next at S113 per cadence S104→S107→S110→S113).\n- In-flight subagent dispatch: empty.\n- Routine system-health survey at S110 close + S111 entry surfaced 4 actionable signals (Q&A pending, drift signals, bash-hook-lint, auto-reboot marker).\n\n---\n\n## T1 — System-health survey (signal triage)\n\n| Signal | Status | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| q-and-a/pending/ | 5 bundles MV'd to answered/ between two `ls` calls (~5s apart) — confirms qa-pending-auto-mover.sh L-S108-1 hour-bucket fix from S109 is operational | NONE — auto-mover working ✓ |\n| bash-hook-lint emissions | 13 sustained baseline (S109 close baseline) — earlier confusion that lint emitted 0 was due to script writing to log+notification, not stdout | NONE — no regression ✓ |\n| Drift signals | Recent: 19 D2-SELF-ATTEST (MEDIUM) on session logs + 1 DR3-LLM-NO-RETRY — routine session-log noise | NONE — known noise pattern ✓ |\n| `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint` marker | **8h+ stale orphan** (mtime 13:47 while checkpoint 360s fresh) — visible in git status as MODIFIED | **T2 fix** below |\n\n---\n\n## T2 — HH-H.1 stale-block marker write-only bug discovery (M-S111-1)\n\n### Discovery\n\n`scripts/session-self-reboot.sh` (HH-H.1 inner fence, 5min strict guard) writes `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint` marker when checkpoint is stale (>300s) → exits 2 to refuse reboot. BUT: **no clear-on-fresh logic**. Marker persists indefinitely after underlying condition (stale checkpoint) resolves.\n\nSibling `scripts/hooks/auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh:64` (HH-H.4 outer fence, 2h soft guard) has the correct pattern:\n```bash\nif [ \"$STALE_AGE_S\" -le \"$STALE_THRESHOLD_S\" ]; then\n  # Checkpoint is fresh; clear any prior pre-block marker so watchdog can fire normally.\n  rm -f \"$PRE_BLOCK_MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n```\n\nHH-H.1 was missing the equivalent clear path. Result: stale orphan markers accumulate on disk and surface as ghost diagnostic state.\n\n### Severity: LOW\n\n- The marker has no active CONSUMER (nothing reads `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint`; it's write-only).\n- HH-H.1 guard re-evaluates from CHECKPOINT mtime each invocation; the marker's presence does NOT block subsequent reboot attempts (unlike HH-H.4's PRE-BLOCK marker which IS consumed by budget-watchdog.sh:78).\n- Bug surface: cosmetic git-status noise + diagnostic confusion (urgent.md content references \"marker `.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint`\" — adjacent but distinct marker name).\n\nDifferent RCA from M-S108-1 (which was per-session-fallback collision); this is missing-clear-path.\n\n### Fix\n\n`scripts/session-self-reboot.sh` Edit (~4 LOC): add comment + `rm -f \"$STALE_BLOCK_MARKER\"` immediately after the guard `fi`. Both branches (FORCE_REBOOT=1 + non-FORCE-fresh-checkpoint) reach this line; both clear the marker. The stale-checkpoint branch exits 2 BEFORE reaching this line (preserving marker on actual abort).\n\n### Smoke test (3 TC manually)\n\n| TC | Setup | Expected | Result |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| TC1 | Plant marker + STOCKFORGE_FORCE_REBOOT=1 | Guard skipped → marker cleared post-fi | PASS |\n| TC2 | Plant marker + checkpoint fresh (touch now) | Guard passes → marker cleared post-fi | PASS |\n| TC3 | Plant marker + checkpoint 600s old | Guard aborts → marker preserved (script exit 2) | PASS |\n\n### Side-effect cleanup\n\nDeleted on-disk `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint` (the 8h-old orphan); git status now shows `D` for the path. Git tracking of the file itself remains a STRUCTURAL ISSUE (file should be `.gitignore`'d like `.auto-reboot-FAILED` is at .gitignore:73 + 82) — filed as backlog item below.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons NEW (S111)\n\nNONE separately codified per AP-23 RED FLAG avoidance. The fix pattern is \"mirror sibling-hook's correct pattern\" — not novel; documented in code via comment \"Mirrors auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh:64 (HH-H.4 sibling fence) clear-on-fresh pattern.\" The DIFFERENT-RCA mistake (M-S111-1) is a single-instance discovery; no lesson-tier rule emerges from one instance.\n\n---\n\n## Mistakes NEW (S111)\n\n**M-S111-1** (digest only; LOW severity) — `session-self-reboot.sh` HH-H.1 stale-block marker write-only / never-clears. BUT: **no clear-on-fresh logic**. Marker persists indefinitely after underlying condition (stale checkpoint) resolves.\n\nSibling `scripts/hooks/auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh:64` (HH-H.4 outer fence, 2h soft guard) has the correct pattern:\n```bash\nif [ \"$STALE_AGE_S\" -le \"$STALE_THRESHOLD_S\" ]; then\n  # Checkpoint is fresh; clear any prior pre-block marker so watchdog can fire normally.\n  rm -f \"$PRE_BLOCK_MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n```\n\nHH-H.1 was missing the equivalent clear path. Result: stale orphan markers accumulate on disk and surface as ghost diagnostic state.\n\n### Severity: LOW\n\n- The marker has no active CONSUMER (nothing reads `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint`; it's write-only).\n- HH-H.1 guard re-evaluates from CHECKPOINT mtime each invocation; the marker's presence does NOT block subsequent reboot attempts (unlike HH-H.4's PRE-BLOCK marker which IS consumed by budget-watchdog.sh:78).\n- Bug surface: cosmetic git-status noise + diagnostic confusion (urgent.md content references \"marker `.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint`\" — adjacent but distinct marker name).\n\nDifferent RCA from M-S108-1 (which was per-session-fallback collision); this is missing-clear-path.\n\n### Fix\n\n`scripts/session-self-reboot.sh` Edit (~4 LOC): add comment + `rm -f \"$STALE_BLOCK_MARKER\"` immediately after the guard `fi`. Both branches (FORCE_REBOOT=1 + non-FORCE-fresh-checkpoint) reach this line; both clear the marker. The stale-checkpoint branch exits 2 BEFORE reaching this line (preserving marker on actual abort).\n\n### Smoke test (3 TC manually)\n\n| TC | Setup | Expected | Result |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| TC1 | Plant marker + STOCKFORGE_FORCE_REBOOT=1 | Guard skipped → marker cleared post-fi | PASS |\n| TC2 | Plant marker + checkpoint fresh (touch now) | Guard passes → marker cleared post-fi | PASS |\n| TC3 | Plant marker + checkpoint 600s old | Guard aborts → marker preserved (script exit 2) | PASS |\n\n### Side-effect cleanup\n\nDeleted on-disk `.auto-reboot-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint` (the 8h-old orphan); git status now shows `D` for the path. Git tracking of the file itself remains a STRUCTURAL ISSUE (file should be `.gitignore`'d like `.auto-reboot-FAILED` is at .gitignore:73 + 82) — filed as backlog item below.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons NEW (S111)\n\nNONE separately codified per AP-23 RED FLAG avoidance. The fix pattern is \"mirror sibling-hook's correct pattern\" — not novel; documented in code via comment \"Mirrors auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh:64 (HH-H.4 sibling fence) clear-on-fresh pattern.\" The DIFFERENT-RCA mistake (M-S111-1) is a single-instance discovery; no lesson-tier rule emerges from one instance.\n\n---\n\n## Mistakes NEW (S111)\n\n**M-S111-1** (digest only; LOW severity) — `session-self-reboot.sh` HH-H.1 stale-block marker write-only / never-clears.
